# Data dictionary

Generated by data/scripts/build_data_dictionary.py. Machine-readable twin: registry/data_dictionary.json (every column of every derived file, including all columns of the all-items Call Report file). This dictionary is summary-free: it reports row counts and which quarters contain an item, never statistics of values.

Loaders: data/scripts/load_derived.py (`load(name)`) and data/scripts/load_derived.R (`load_derived(name)`) read the Parquet twin by default and the CSV with `fmt="csv"`; column types come from registry/derived_schema.json. See registry/loader_check.md.

General conventions

- Call Report dollar items are in thousands of U.S. dollars. Balance-sheet items (Schedule RC and components) are end-of-quarter stocks; Schedule RC-K items (codes 33xx-34xx) are quarterly averages; income-statement items (RIAD) are calendar year-to-date flows (the fourth-quarter report holds the full year).
- Mnemonics: RCFD consolidated (domestic plus foreign offices); RCON domestic offices; RCFN foreign offices; RIAD income statement; RSSD structure. On these files RCFD is populated for all filers and equals RCON for banks without foreign offices.
- Reporting forms during 1984-1993: FFIEC 031 (banks with foreign offices), 032, 033 and 034 (domestic-only banks by asset size). Smaller-bank forms collect fewer details, so some component items are missing for those banks. The March 1984 revision introduced new items (e.g., 0390 total investment securities, 2146 trading account assets, 1766 C&I excluding acceptances); 1983Q4 follows the pre-1984 layout. Separate fed funds sold (0276) and reverse repo (0277) items have values only from 1988Q1; before that only the combined item 1350 exists. Held-to-maturity/available-for-sale securities (1754/1773) start in 1994 and are empty here.
- Missing values: provider missing values are kept as missing; nothing is imputed. In CSVs an empty field is missing.
- Candidate item lists per concept (assets by category, average assets, deposits, income and expense) are in registry/call_item_candidates.json; they are an inventory of alternatives, not a selection.

## call_mnwi_allitems_1983q4_1993q4.csv.gz

- Path: data/derived/call_mnwi_allitems_1983q4_1993q4.csv.gz (Parquet twin: data/derived/call_mnwi_allitems_1983q4_1993q4.parquet)
- Rows: 49046; columns: 3279
- Unique key: quarter, RSSD9001
- Produced by: data/scripts/extract_call_mnwi.py
- Content: Every record in the Chicago Fed quarterly Call Report files 1983Q4-1993Q4 whose physical state (RSSD9210) is Minnesota (27) or Wisconsin (55); every provider column kept (union over quarters).
- Notes: All charter types, entity types and reporting levels are retained. Columns absent from a quarter's provider file are missing for that quarter (see call_item_presence_by_quarter.csv). Per-column definitions: data/derived/call_items_mdrm_dictionary.csv and the JSON twin of this dictionary.

Column groups (per-column definitions, units, item codes and coverage for all columns are in registry/data_dictionary.json and data/derived/call_items_mdrm_dictionary.csv):

| group | columns | definition source |
|---|---|---|
| identifiers added | 2 | this dictionary |
| provider identifiers | 3 | MDRM |
| structure (RSSD) | 54 | MDRM |
| RCFD | 1022 | MDRM |
| RCON | 1675 | MDRM |
| RCFN | 31 | MDRM |
| RIAD | 492 | MDRM |

## call_item_presence_by_quarter.csv

- Path: data/derived/call_item_presence_by_quarter.csv (Parquet twin: data/derived/call_item_presence_by_quarter.parquet)
- Rows: 134357; columns: 5
- Unique key: item, quarter
- Produced by: data/scripts/extract_call_mnwi.py
- Content: Item x quarter grid: whether the item exists in the provider file (in_file), whether any MN/WI row has a non-missing value (any_nonmissing_mnwi), and whether any MN/WI row has a non-zero value (any_nonzero_mnwi).
- Notes: Flags are 0/1 coverage indicators only.

| column | type | units | definition | source item | coverage | caveats |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| item | string | see definition | Provider column name (e.g., RCFD2170). | derived file column | all rows |  |
| quarter | string | see definition | YYYYQn. | derived file column | all rows |  |
| in_file | double | see definition | 1 if the column exists in that quarter's provider file, else 0. | derived file column | all rows |  |
| any_nonmissing_mnwi | double | see definition | 1 if at least one MN/WI row has a non-missing value, else 0. | derived file column | all rows |  |
| any_nonzero_mnwi | double | see definition | 1 if at least one MN/WI row has a non-missing, non-zero (or non-blank text) value, else 0. | derived file column | all rows |  |

| column | type | units | definition | source item | coverage | caveats |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| quarter | string | see definition | YYYYQn. | derived file column | all rows |  |
| rows_in_file | double | see definition | Rows in the national provider file (all states). | derived file column | all rows |  |
| cols_in_file | double | see definition | Columns in the provider file. | derived file column | all rows |  |
| rows_mn_wi | double | see definition | Rows kept (RSSD9210 in 27, 55). | derived file column | all rows |  |

## call_items_mdrm_dictionary.csv

- Path: data/derived/call_items_mdrm_dictionary.csv (Parquet twin: data/derived/call_items_mdrm_dictionary.parquet)
- Rows: 3279; columns: 10
- Unique key: column
- Produced by: data/scripts/derive_extracts.py
- Content: Per-column dictionary for the all-items Call Report file: MDRM item name, item type, forms, MDRM validity dates, quarter coverage on the files and among MN/WI rows, whether the column is in the core file, and the MDRM description (truncated to 2,000 characters).
- Notes: MDRM validity dates describe the dictionary entry and can disagree with actual presence on the historical files; the coverage columns describe actual presence.

| column | type | units | definition | source item | coverage | caveats |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| column | string | see definition | Column name in the all-items file. | derived file column | all rows |  |
| mdrm_item_name | string | see definition | MDRM item name. | derived file column | all rows |  |
| mdrm_item_type | string | see definition | MDRM item type (F financial/reported, D derived, R rate, P percentage, S structure, ...). | derived file column | all rows |  |
| mdrm_forms | string | see definition | Reporting forms listed in MDRM for this identifier. | derived file column | all rows |  |
| mdrm_start | string | see definition | Earliest MDRM start date. | derived file column | all rows |  |
| mdrm_end | string | see definition | Latest MDRM end date ('open' if ongoing). | derived file column | all rows |  |
| quarters_in_file | string | see definition | First..last quarter in which the column exists in the provider files (number of quarters). | derived file column | all rows |  |
| quarters_with_any_value_mnwi | string | see definition | First..last quarter with any non-missing MN/WI value (number of quarters). | derived file column | all rows |  |
| in_core_file | double | see definition | 1 if the column is also in the core file. | derived file column | all rows |  |
| mdrm_description | string | see definition | MDRM description text (truncated). | derived file column | all rows |  |

## fdic_institutions_mnwi_and_linked.csv

- Path: data/derived/fdic_institutions_mnwi_and_linked.csv (Parquet twin: data/derived/fdic_institutions_mnwi_and_linked.parquet)
- Rows: 1802; columns: 140
- Unique key: CERT
- Produced by: data/scripts/derive_extracts.py
- Content: FDIC BankFind institution records: all institutions whose current or last location is MN or WI, plus institutions linked by FDIC certificate from the Call Report extract or the structure events.

## fdic_structure_events_mnwi_1983_1994.csv

- Path: data/derived/fdic_structure_events_mnwi_1983_1994.csv (Parquet twin: data/derived/fdic_structure_events_mnwi_1983_1994.parquet)
- Rows: 5466; columns: 174
- Unique key: ID
- Produced by: data/scripts/derive_extracts.py
- Content: FDIC BankFind history (structure change) events with effective date 1983-01-01..1994-12-31 involving an institution, office or former location in MN or WI: mergers, absorptions, consolidations, failures, new charters, name/location/charter/regulator changes, branch acquisitions and sales.
- Notes: CHANGECODE/CHANGECODE_DESC give the event type; ACQ_* = acquirer, OUT_* = institution merged out, SUR_* = survivor, FRM_* = prior values. Raw file covers 1980-1996.

## deflators_long.csv

- Path: data/derived/deflators_long.csv (Parquet twin: data/derived/deflators_long.parquet)
- Rows: 5109; columns: 9
- Unique key: provider, series_id, source_file, date, bls_period
- Produced by: data/scripts/derive_extracts.py
- Content: All price-index observations downloaded, long format (one row per series x source file x date).
- Notes: Series: CPIAUCSL (CPI-U all items, U.S. city average, seasonally adjusted, monthly, 1982-84=100); CPIAUCNS (same, not seasonally adjusted); GDPDEF (GDP implicit price deflator, quarterly, SA, current base year per FRED page); A191RD3A086NBEA (GDP implicit price deflator, annual); CUURA211SA0 (FRED copy of a discontinued BLS CPI-U all items series for Minneapolis-St. Paul, NSA, observations through 1986-12); CUUSA211SA0 (annual Minneapolis-St. Paul-Bloomington CPI-U from 1984, NSA); ALFRED GDPDEF real-time vintages (base years differ by vintage); BLS API: CUUR0000SA0, CUSR0000SA0 (U.S. city average NSA/SA), CUURS24ASA0 (Minneapolis-St. Paul-Bloomington NSA, monthly/bimonthly as published), CUUSS24ASA0 (same area, semiannual). Annual averages are provided only where FRED computed them (frequency column); no averages were computed here.

| column | type | units | definition | source item | coverage | caveats |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| provider | string | see definition | FRED, ALFRED or BLS API v2. | derived file column | all rows |  |
| series_id | string | see definition | Series identifier at the provider. | derived file column | all rows |  |
| source_file | string | see definition | Raw file path (relative to the study folder). | derived file column | all rows |  |
| vintage | string | see definition | Vintage/release label recorded at download. | derived file column | all rows |  |
| frequency | string | see definition | Frequency of the observation as provided (or FRED annual aggregation). | derived file column | all rows |  |
| date | string | see definition | Observation date (period start, YYYY-MM-DD). | derived file column | all rows |  |
| bls_period | string | see definition | BLS period code (Mnn month, S01/S02 half-year, S03 annual). | derived file column | all rows |  |
| value | double | see definition | Index value as published (index points; base period per series documentation). | derived file column | all rows |  |
| footnotes | string | see definition | BLS footnote text, if any. | derived file column | all rows |  |

- Notes: Change notes for 1984-1990 (mach8490.txt) and the 1993 update (maupdate.txt) are in the raw folder; they contain no change to area 5120. Central/outlying flag is not given in the 1981 file.
